Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the electrochemistry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 619. The moduli space has dimension at most 980 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the electrochemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^619 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 980-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
